# Spring PetClinic — Complete Commit Size Index

**Main GitHub Timestamps reference.** Sorted by changed source-code lines, smallest first. The History # column preserves the topological order needed for replay.

- **Upstream:** [https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-petclinic](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-petclinic)
- **Pinned main commit:** [`500158f732419217507c7656904b8e6aa1bcc0d6`](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-petclinic/tree/500158f732419217507c7656904b8e6aa1bcc0d6)
- **Commit rows:** **1,047 / 1,047**, unique and audited
- **Total code-line changes:** **549,703** (+248,374 / -301,329)
- **All textual changes:** **588,404**; binary file changes: 981
- **Sequencing counts:** Git-based 751, Hybrid 185, Logical 111

## Definitions and audit rules

1. **Code Δ** = Code + (added lines) + Code − (deleted lines). This is the ascending sort key; ties use file count, UTC date and SHA.
2. **Files** = all files changed relative to the first parent, including non-code and binaries. Rename detection is enabled: a pure rename counts as one file.
3. **Code files** = files in source, test, templates, stylesheets, SQL, XML, build/config, or script extensions. Blank lines and comments count. README and plain documentation do not count as code.
4. **All + / All − / All Δ** include changes in non-code text files too. Binaries count as changed files but contribute zero text lines.
5. **Merge commits** are compared to their first parent; all commits reachable from pinned main are included once. Commit dates are converted to UTC.
6. **Mode**: Git-based for Code Δ up to 50 and Files up to 3; Hybrid for Code Δ up to 300 and Files up to 7; otherwise Logical. Modes are reconstructed teaching strategies, not original typing history.
7. **Audit**: exactly 1,047 unique commit IDs, complete pinned ancestry, two independent Git diff methods for the changed-file count, ascending sort, and head cross-checked against GitHub REST (4 files, +36/−2). Generation fails on any mismatch.
8. **Source date vs simulation:** dates below are real commit dates, while D01/D02 timestamps in the UI will be simulated.

## Reproducibility

Generated from the full upstream Git object history by the project's [GitHub Actions index builder](https://github.com/sabareeshrao/Gradstudio-Version-2.0/blob/main/.github/workflows/github-timestamps-petclinic-index.yml).
The sorted table is an index, not a chronological animation: use History # and each commit's diff to build the actual D01/D02 playback.
